*KANO*

Yana Katse wayar yaja Motar a hankali, Bai tsaya ko ina ba sai Cikin wani Sabon estate  ( *Summer Estate* ) .

Estate ne hadadde Wanda ya amsa sunanshi estate, Dukda Sabon Estate ne Amma Daidaikun Gidaje ne Suke Empty.

Estate din shiru kamar babu mutane aciki , Sai dai haushin Karnukan da ke Wanzuwa Jefi jefi.

Gidajene Jere duk iri daya.
Bazaka iya banbancesuba saika duba number dake liqe a kowanne kofar gidan kasancewar gidajen Jere suke according to their numbers Kowanne gida an kewayeshi da furranni masu kyau ,Da Abubuwa mabanbanta masu ban sha'awa.

Ta madubi ya hangi mai gadi yana Kiran Waya,ko ba'a gaya masa ba yasan Mutan Gidan yake Sanar ma Zuwansa.

Gajeren tsaki yaja a ransa yana Jinjina halin Mutan Gidan ba masu Gidan ba hatta ma'aikatan Gidan halinsu ɗaya (wato Gulma).

Jakarsa  ya ɗauka ya fito ya kulle Motar sa, Ya tunkari Ciki Gadan gadan, Cikin wani irin ƙasaitaccen taku.

Bai ida ƙarasa Shiga ba yaji saukar wheat Flour A jikinshi daga Sama.

'daga Kan sa yayi don yaga daga ina Flour Din ke saukowa Amma ya kasa Ganin komai Sakamakon Rufewa da Idanunsa sukayi, Saurin Saukar dakansa yayi ya Cire Tabarau ɗinsa yana zazzare Idanu.

inji Driver aladabce Saidai deep down dariya yake mishi.

A chan kanon dabo kuwa Su Saleem na dawowa Suka ga su zee a yashe a kasa Suna fidda numfashi dakyar,Saurin rushing dinsu Akayi zuwa Near by clinic Aka basu taimakon gaggawa,Koda aka tambayesu Abinda ya faru cewa Sukayi sun Shiga Bathroom din Yaa Aash ne Shocking yajasu Daga Nan basu sake sanin Inda Suke ba ,Ko Kadan Umman su Bata yarda ba Dan tasan Abin da ta sasu,ta kumaga Mood dinshi a lkcn a Haka ma yayi msu sauqi, ta godewa Allah ma da ba kakkarya su yayi ba.

Tun Daga Ranar Babu wani Abinda ya Shiga tsakaninsu na Rashin 'da'a Haka sauran Ni'ima da Naja Duk sun Kama kansu.

Yau ma kamar Kullum kamar yanda ya Saba Yana Nan dakin Goggo(kakarsa) suna Hira,bayan Aeesh itace ta mutum biyu da yafi sabawa da ita, sannan Mai Aikin Goggon Wato Inna Tani ,Yana matukar girmamata Sosai idan ka kikaga yanda yake Bata girma sai kiyi zaton Mahaifiyarsa ce, ko Mahaifinsa baya bashi kwatankwacin girman da yake Bata .

Yau kwanakin Sa biyar da suwa kano Zaune suke a dakin Goggo, Yana Taya inna Tani yankar danyen Ku'bewa, Goggo na Tsokanarsa da cewa Yau har Tuwon Shi zai Tuka dariya ya dingayi yana Fadin ai ba Yau ya fara Tuka tuwo ba su Saleem ne irin sai Sun jira mace ta girka kafin su ci, aikam kamar ance ya Rufe baki sai jiyo sallamar Saleem Sukaji, Amsawa Duk sukayi.

sake had'e rai Aashman yayi , kana ya cigaba da yankar Kubewar, hakan ba karamin tunzura Goggo yayi ba take ta shiga kumfar baki

A'isha (Aeesh) tana da kyau, irin kyawun nan mai tafiya da zuciya lokaci guda, komai na ta Daidai kamar ita ta hallici Kanta, tanada Yanayin Sanyi sosai irin na marasa hayaniyan  don kallon farko sai kayi tunanin Irin Masu Ji da kan nan ne ko daya daga cikin ire iren characters na Novels dinan ne masu masifar iyayin tsiya.

saidai a zahiri ba haka bane don ita din mutum ce mai sanyin hali da mu'amala barta dai da tsiwa da Shirme.

_Reey ki fahimceni, bazan iya rayuwa batareda shi ba Shi ɗin Rayuwana ne_  Kalamanta na dazu suka dawo Masa,Huci mai zafi ya furzar yana Sake Tunano Kalaman Abbansa  _Mun Riga Mun Gama komai har Sadaki mun kai Ranar kadai muke jira_

Hakan yasa ta tsorata Sosai Ta Rungumeshi tana ihu.

Ji yayi ya Kasa ko motsawa , jikinshi yayi liqis jiyake kamar babu sauran lakka ajikinshi, hannu na rawa ya ɓanɓarota daga Riƙon da tayi masa,Ya fito Sannan ya zagaya ya bude Mata ta fito Jikinta babu inda baya rawa har yanzu a tsorace take,Bai damu ba ya buɗe Booth Ɗin motar ya dauko Basket a booth, Hannunta yaja Suka Shige Cikin Asibitin tsoro fal a ranta, tunda take dashi bata taɓa Ganinshi Cikin irin wannan Yanayin ba Shiyasa ta tsorata Sosai.

inji Aeesh Cikin tsokana

Da sallama Suka Shigo.

Bathroom ya faɗa Saida yayi wanka ya gudanar da sallar Isha'i da ya riga ya wuce shi ,Sannan yayi shirin barci ya haye gado ya kwanta Ya kwanta Gefen ta ,Baici Abinci ba kuma ko kadan bayajin yunwa. 

Kwanciya yayi shiru idanunshi akan p.o.p. 

Yakasa yarda haryanzu wai shi Ango ne, Angon Aeesh ,Yau Shi ya Auri Aeesh ,just like that? Saikuma ya tuna Shekaran Jiya Suka je aka Saka Ranar Bikinta da Aareef.

Murmushi yadanyi, tunowa yayi da yanayin da ya ganta ɗazu Abin tausayi da Kuma Yanda Baffa ke roƙonsa Da ya Amince a daura auren,Take Murmushinsa Ya disashe ya fara tunanin dalilin da zaisa baffa ya buƙaci Ya aura masa ita cikin gaggawa.

Da yaga Ashman Da Saleem Sun fara Wayau ,Sun haɗe kansu In yana Kuka sai Su daukeshi Suyita masa Wasa ,Haka naseer Shima tare Suke Wasa ,Sai ya sauya Masu gida.

Mahaifiyar Ashman da Ashman dinta Shi kadai ,Sai kuma Saude mai reno Da Ashraf, Saleem ,Naseer da Abduljaleel.

Saude kam ji tayi sam batajin daɗin Zaman Nigeria Nan ma tada tsiya tayi Akan zata koma Turkish yarda Abba Yayi ta Koma   ita kadai saboda A lokacin ya kusa Kammala Ginin babban Asibitinsa Da yake Ginawa a nan Cikin Garin kaduna yana Ida Ginawa ya damƙama Haj hassana Takardun Tace na ɗanta Ashman ne in ya girma a lokacin yanada Shekaru Goma da haihuwa ,Tun yana Ƙan kaninshi ya taso da Burin Zama Likita ,haj Hussaina Tayi mamaki Sosai Duba da Rashin mutuncin da Yake nuna msu na iya Shekarun da suka gabata lokaci Guda yayi ma 'dansa wannan Gagarumin Kyautar Ai abin da Mamaki.

Tunda Nooriyyah Ta haifi Sadeeq bata sake samun haihuwa ba sai bayan shekaru Goma a lokacin su Ashman Sun dawo Nigeria ,Inda Allah ya nufeta da Haihuwar 'yan Biyu duk mata ,a ranar da ta haihu Faisal ya diro Nigeria da zunmar Auren Rabin Ransa Bayan ya shafe shekaru da dama ya Tara maƙuden Kudade a ranar ya iske Ta haihu.

Yayi baƙinciki sosai ya kuma kuduri aniyar Daukar fansa Ko ta wane hali Satinsa ɗaya a ƙasar yana Jinyar zuciyarsa.

A ranar suna Yara Sukaci Sunan Aisha Humayrah da Kuma Surayyah Ana tsaka da Gudanar da bikin Suna Aka nemi suree aka Rasa Faisal ne ya saceta da Niyyar daukar fansa Kuma Ko babu komai Ganin gudar jinin abar kaunarsa Zai rage masa radadin Rashinta a Zuciyarsa, a ranar ya bar Ƙasar da ita ya nema Mata Nani mai Reno a chan UK inda yake gudanar da harkar Kasuwancinsa.

Aeesh ta taso Cikin Gata na iyayenta dama dangi gaba ɗaya ta koina sonta ake Barin ma Ashman ,Koda aka Yayeta Mahaifiyar Ashman ta Roƙi alfarma Gun ɗan uwanta da ya bata Aeesh ta hadasu da kanwar Ashman wato Hafsah, a tare suka taso Cikin ƙauna da Kulawar yayansu Ashman ,har yanzu Saude na chan Turkish Itada Su Saleem ko Ziyara basuayi Saidai Abba yaje In yana Less busy yaga yaransa ya dawo.

Aeesh nada Shekaru Uku a duniya aka haifo mata Ƙani wato Habeebullah Wanda a yanzu Suke kira Habeeb.

Arzikin Dr Hassan Na Ƙara bunƙasa Yana Ƙara shahara Habeeb nada Shekaru biyu Shima ya mallaki nasa Asibitin Ido anan cikin garin kaduna,A ashekarar kuma Sukaje Wani Gasa a Egypt anan y samu kyautar wata gagarumin Kyauta kyautar companyn Milo daga wani Dan kaduna da ya halacci gasar, Sosai Dr hassan Ya burgeshi yanda Ya cinye gasar ƙasa da ƙasar da Aka gudanar Tsakaninsa da Turawan Wasu ƙasashe.

Yana dawowa Tasa Matsalar Ta fara ,Kyautar da aka bashi ya damƙawa Matarsa Noor Wato mahaifiyar Aeesh.

A lokacin Dangi ya rikice ,Haka kawai gaba da hassada ya shiga tsakaninsu daga nan suka ƙaurewa Zumunci ,Ajman dake chan Kasar Egypt a da yana Leƙowa Amma yanzu ko ɗan Phone call dinnan Ba suayi dashi, A halin yanzu Yaransa biyu duk Sun Girma.

Ana tsaka da wannan Saude ta tattaro Inata inata ta dawo Nigeria lokacin Ashman Ya samu Admission yana Karatu a KASU.

Gabar Dake tsakanin dangi yayi tsamarinda har yakai Ga Suna Gudun Juna Haka kawai rana Tsaka Dr. Naseer ya tattara iyalansa Suka koma kano da Zama, Ashman Dake Kaduna yana karatu Baya komawa gida sai bayan shekara.

A lokacin Ƙaunar dake tsakaninsa Da 'yan uwansa ya tarwatse babu mai ganin Girmansa dukda shine babba ,A fili su Nafeesah ke nuna matsa Tsana bai taɓa nuna ya damu ba tunda yana Tareda su Saleem atleast he's not alone kaman da.

Ana haka Dr hassan da Yar uwansa Hussaina suka tafi Umrah ,Lokacin Ashman ya zama likita, Saleem kuma yana Turkish yana Nasa karatun , rana ɗaya suka dawo ,Ashman ne yaje ɗaukosu a filin jirgi.

Anan Filin Jirgin aka samu wasu marasa Imanin Da suka ƙona Dr Hassan Da Yar uwarsa Hussaina.

Rasuwarsu y gigita kowanne a dangin Dukda Rashin Jituwan dake tsakaninsu ,haƙiƙa Sunyi babban rashi .

Tun daga ranar Gaba mai tsanani ya shiga tsakanin Ashman da saleem sakamakon Abinda ya gani a zahiri yana Zargin Saleem da kashe masa Mahaifiya.

Yaran Baffa Imam uku Sumayya itace babba,Sai Ummulkhayri da Idris, Sumayya tayi Aure Ummulkhari da idris sun gama Secondary sch Suna jiran Admission ne .

*_Back to the Story.._*

Baffa ,Umma da idris aka kashe Mahaifiyarsu kuma Akayi mata illa....

Innalillahi Wainnailaihirraji'un kadai Ashman Ke ta maimaitawa, yayinda Kukan Aeesh ya daɗu, cewa take _"Wlhy Su Bamalli ne, Sune Suka Kashe Mn Baffa na"_

Hankalinsa a matuƙar tashe ya ɗau Makullin Motarsa ya fice ,da gudu ta mara masa baya tna kuka ,Bai hanata ba don bazai iya barinta a gida ita kaɗai ,Motar ya shiga ya tada bai tsaya ko ina ba sai gidan baffa.

Allahu Akbar Baffa imam Allah sarki Umma da Idris ,Allah sarki Ammy Duk zube suke cikin Jini .

cikin wani irin firgita da gigicewa da fita hankalin Aeesh ta tasaki wani irin kara mai firgatar da duk wani bani Adam dake wurin,
juyowa Ashman yayi da ƙarfi ya daka mata tsawa Shima hankalin nasa a  tashe yKe ya ma  rasa ta ina zai fara .

"Wayyo Allah na wayyo Baffa na Wayyoni Aisha naga ta kaina ,Bamalli me Abbana yayi maka ka kashe shi?,Nice na maka lefi ,Nice na cantanci na Mutu".

sai ƙara ruggumoshi a jikinta  take ta rik'eshi gam tana kuka mai ban tausayi.

'dai bayan daya Ashman ya dinga daukansu yana kaisu Cikin Mota da ƙyar ya samu ya raba baffa da Aeesh.

Shiga motar yayi ya figeshi a guje a lokacin karfe 2:36am.

Asibitinsa Suka nufa Suna tun kan Su iso Yayi Alarming yan sanda so basu samu matsala dasu a hanya ba.

Emergency Unit aka shiga dasu ,nan aka tabbatar da Rasuwar Idris da Ummulkhayri baffa da Ammi suna raye.

Taimakon Gaggawa likitoci suka shiga basu, Cikin Ƙanƙanin lokaci aka samo Numfashin Ammie ,baffa kuwa A saitin zuciyarsa aka daɓa masa Wuƙa Da kyar ya rayu.

Ana sama Baffa Oxygen Wutan Asibitin ya ɗauke abinda bai taɓa faruwa ba a tarihin asibitin.

Ambu bag aka samu akayi ta matsa masa a hanci ko Numfashin nasa Zai dawo Amma ina ko gezau,Gashi pulse Dinsa na bugawa a hankali a hankali.

Banda kuka babu Abinda Aeesh keyi gani take idan baffa ya rasu bazata Yafewa kanta ba, itace silar shigansa wannan yanayi da batayi ma bamalli Alƙawari ba da yanzu Baffa na zaune cikin Ƙoshin lafiya.

Idanunta takai kan oxometer taga Pulse rate dinsa na raguwa da gudu ta Ƙarasa gadon da Aka shimfidashi Ko ina na jikinsa jone da Na'Urori dukda bbu wuta amma ko wanne na gudanar da aaikinsa kadan kaɗan.

Hannunta ta dungule ta kai masa Duka Daidai Saitin ƙirjinsa.

A razane likitocin Suka ɗago......

Duk yanda Aeesh taso ta chanzawa Umma asibiti hakan ya gagara ,Aash ya tsaya kan Komai ya tsare ya kasa bai yarda Koda wasa Tasamu privilege Dinnan ba.

Bayan Wasu Yan gwaje Gwajen da likitoci Suka gudanar akan Umma Sun Gano Jininta ne yayi mugun Hawa wanda hakan Yasata Samu shanyewar ɓarin Jiki haka Kidney dinta Yaso Ya taɓa, odarta Ta dama Ta fara samun Rauni wato(Acute Renal failure).

Tun ɗazu su Bamalli suka Shigo Asibitin suna neman ta Amma basu Ganta ba  Kasancewar Asibitin nada girma sosai,Sun Zagaye Ƙananun Wards inda Ake kwantar da Manya Amma basu ga koda mai kaman ta ba, Fitowar su kenan Daga PNC har sunyi hanyar fita, ɗaya daga cikin tawagar shi ya hango mutum a zaune ta kife kanta Tana kuka,Tabo Bamalli yayi Yana Nuna masa Ita.

"kasan wake daga hannu akan mace da niyyar duka? ,Bunsuru ,Bunsuru shine yake bin Mace ako wane lokaci Batareda ya damu da Yanayin da take Ciki ba, Shidai buƙatarsa saƙonsa sa ya isa inda yake son isarwa, inada masaniya Akan Duk abinda kake aikatawa , mahaifiyarka itace take Goya maka baya akan Komai Shiyasa Baka dauki mace Wani abu ba,ita kanta mahaifiyar taka bata cika mace ba Shiyasa bata san darajar mace ba,Ahto macen Da bata san Mutuncin yar uwanta mace ba, bata damu da Halin da Zata shiga idan Aka ɓata Rayuwar yar uwanta ba,Ita dai burinta danta ya samu Farinciki,bata damu idan wani ya rasa Rayuwansa ta sanadiyyar Samun FarinCikin Danta ba,Irin Wa'ennan Matayen ba Mata bane su ɗin Uwaye ne don Sun haifa ,amma haihuwar Bai Amfanar dasu da Komai ba sai Taɓewa ,wasu Kuma haihuwar shike kaisu jahannama,Wlhy bambali karka ga na mareka Kayi Tunanin Iya nan karfina ya tsaya, Idan ka sake Tunkarar inda Take ko ka sake hanya ta sake haɗa ku Saina Kakkarya Ƙasussuwan ka , Saina yi maka Raunin Da har ka mutu bazaka manta dani Ba,ku Kuma na dawo gareku"

“sunanshi muzzammil,Amma Ana kiransa Da Bamalli Dan mallawa ne, Shine tantirin nan da Muke baka labarin Saura kadan Yayi mana Fyaɗe ran nan,inba don kiyayewar Uban Giji ba da yanzu muna nan an Lalata mana Rayuwa,Allah ya tsare Mu daga Sharrinsa Amma ban san dalilin da yasa ya dawo ba"

"Eh Gidansu ba Boyayye bane , billionaire City yake, Amma fa basu kwana A gida daga shi har Uwansa ,SUN fi ganewa Kwana a hotels mahaifinsa kuwa baya zaman ƙasar, Shiyasa ya maida Gidan Nasu tamkar Club Ko bariki wajen baje hajar mata sam Bashida Daraja Ko kadan"

Saleem ya wani murmushi wanda shi kadai yasan Ma'anar Abinsa yana sake tsatstsarenta da idanunsa farare tas, a hankali ya furta, 

“Inasonki da yawa Aisha ta, Ko kadan Bana son ganin Wannan Hawayen naki, A duk lokacin da na gansu ji nake kamar wanke Zuciyata kike da ruwan Maliya, gara dai nayi na mallakeki kusa dani na huta da Distance ɗin nan da ace na Aureki yanzu nasan abinda zanyi Miki In Hana  Hawayen ki zuba”.

Ya ƙarshe maganar da kai hannu fuskarta da niyyar goge mata Hawaye, ,ji yayi an riƙe Hannun nashi da karfi.


Gaba ɗaya jijiyoyin Kan Ashman sun mimmiƙe ruɗu-ruɗu, numfashinsa sai kaikawo yake  saboda tsabar  fidda Huci mai tsananin Zafi.

 Ya runtse idanunsa tare da matse hannun Saleem, muryarsa a matuƙar kausashe yace

"Don't you Ever try that Rubbish, da kayi Kuskuren Taɓa fuskar ta ta sa kayi mamakin yanda zan daddatsa yatsunka na watsa ma karnuka"

Tsawa Abba ya daka msu, haka yasa shi Sakin Hannunshi yana Huci, Shima saleem Din Huci yake yi yana Kallon Cikin Idon Aaah,idanunsa sun sake kaɗawa sunyi masifar yin jazurr, jijiyoyin kansa sunyi ruɗu-ruɗu. 

habeeb sai sharar ƙwalla yakeyi ganin tashin Hankalin da Yar Uwarsa ke ciki ga mahaifiyarsu a kwance Amma Suna zancen Aurar da Yar uwarsa ga wanda bai san darajar Mace ba?, wannan Wane irin Rashin imani ne?.

Ɗagowa Aash yay yana kallonsu, dagani kasan shima kukan zuci yakeyi, ya maida dubansa ga Abba Cikin Bacin rai idanunsa na sake kaɗawa suna sake komawa jajur fiye da da.


"Abba ina Matuƙar Girmama ka a matsayin ka na Uba,haka Kai ma Baffa ,idan ba so kuke In Aiwatar da abin da zai zo ya dame mu Gabaki daya ba ,ku daga Bikin nan har sai Ammie taji sauƙi, Kuma ku Bari Aeesh ta zaɓi abokin Rayuwarta da kanta"


"A'isha Ni Takeso ko yanzu aka tambaye ta zata tabbatar muku da gaskiya,Aure mu da rashinsa Bazaiba mara lafiya lafiya ba"

Kallon Aeesh Ashman Yayi a kasashe yace

"Ihkty Da gaske kina son Saleem?"

Ɗagowa tayi ta dubi saleem, Ko Kallonta baiyi ba ya kauda kai gefe.

Bayan hannu tasa ta share Kwalla da ya Gangaro mata ,Cikin sanyi tace

"Ina Son Yaya Salee..."

Wani irin Damƙa yakai wa hannayenta biyu, ya zuba mata manyan idanunsa da sukai jazur da ɓacin rai yace

"Ƙarya kike Aees bakya sonshi "

"Ina sonshi "

Ta fada cikin hawaye

Saleem dake kallonsu idanu jazur ya ɗauke kansa daga garesu a karo na farko, murya a dakushe yace

"Kome zaka yi bazaka iya hana Aurennan ba, Nobody is against It"

 "Shut up"

Ya faɗa cikin daka tsawa yana ɗaga hannu tamkar zai make Aeesh dake gabanshi.

 Baya tayi da sauri tana rumtse idanu dan tama sadaƙar mazgeta zaiyi.

Kasa cewa komai yayi, sai jikinsa dake wani irin rawar bala'i.

"Ihkty karki yarda ki Aureshi , Bazai iya kula dake ba, kin manta ke amana ta ce , Ni nakeda Alhakin Nema miki Abokin Rayuwa Sam Saleem Bai Dace dake ba"

Ya fada Cikin Rawar murya kamar Zaiyi kuma ƙwanjinsa da jijiyoyin kansa na sake buɗewa, yanda ƙirjinsa ke sama da ƙasa kawai zai tabbatar maka zuciyarsa na gudu maiban tsoro dake neman fin ƙarfin gangangar jikin nasa.


Daga Irin rikon da yayi ma Hannunta ma ya isa ya tabbatar mata da Tsananin ƙunar da yakeji a zuciyarshi ba.

 Sosai Taji zuciyarta ta karaya Amma  duk da haka bata ji zata iya Sadaukar da mutuncinta da rayuwar mahaifiyanta Akan auren Saleem ba.....

*019*



Sosai Taji zuciyarta ta karaya Amma  duk da haka bata ji zata iya Sadaukar da mutuncinta da rayuwar mahaifiyanta Akan auren Saleem ba.

Rintse Idanu tayi ,ta sake maimaitawa

"Kayi haƙuri Arhy Ina Son Yaa Saleem"

Ta ƙarshe maganar Muryarta na cracking.

Sakin Hannunta yayi A hankali jikinsa a sanyaye ,idanunsa jajur ta ɗago ta kalle saleem, shima kallon nashi yake, Murmushi Fall a fuskar sa, 

Shima Ashman murmushi yayi ya tako gabanshi, 

A hankali Ashman ya sauke idonsa akan Saleem har zuwa mutanen dakin ya maida Idanunsa kan Aeesh dake ƙoƙarin hana Hawayen Idanun ta Diga tamkar ba ita ba, sake maida kallonshi yayi ga Saleem a wani yanayi mai nuni da tsantsar Dakewa da jarumta.

Ashman yasa Hannu cikin Aljihu ya zaro Farar medicated Glass dinsa a aljihunsa ya rufe idanunsa sannan Ya tura hannayensa a aljihu duka biyun yana ja da baya fuskarsa na ƙara tsukewa.

"A duk lokacin da mutum mai idanu ya saka Farar Medicated Glass yana da matuƙar Ƙayatarwa, ta wane Bangaren Kuwa ,Yana Ƙarawa mutum karfin Ganin Idanu, wasu lokutan har kakan hango Abubuwan da Mai lafiyayyun Idanu baya hangowa saboda karfin da Bature ya Bama Gilashin, amma da tsautsayi zaisa mara lafiyan ido wanda Aka bashi Gilashin don Kara Kaifin Ganin (kaman Ni dai) ,Yayi gangancin Yin Mugun tseren da zaisa Gilashin ya fashe Zai iya shiga Alhinin har yayi Tunanin chanza wani, shi kuma gilashin da ya fashe kokarinka kawai Ka yar da Fasassun Gudun kar yayi maka illa, wanda ya Tarwatse a ƙasa kuwa sai kaga ana Kafa kafa Don Kar a taka ,musamman idan ya kasance irin Nawa mai d'auke da asalin Gilashi, Wanda zai iya Jawa Mutum Rauni Ko tayaya ya taɓa a fashe, glass Kuma Tsalle yake ya fada inda ba'a zato,  Saleem !, Bansan Ya Ka daukeni a zuciyar ka ba, amma Ni daukar da nayi maka Daidai yake da Glass Dinnan a Rayuwata ,Idan Kana tareda Ni nakan Hango Abinda Kai kanka baka hangowa a tattare da kai, Karka Sake Nayi tseren da zaisa Gilashin Ta kuɓuce ta faɗi ta Tarwatse ,Domin Kuwa koda ya Tarwatse na yarda Gangar Jikin Bazan bari Ragowar Fasassun Suyi Illa Ga Na kusa dani ba,Bansan Ya zaka dau zance na ba, Amma Ina umurtan Ka da ka gaggauta Kwance Kullalliyar da ka Kulla a zancen Aurenka da Ihkty...!"

Shiru dakin Ya dauka, Gabaki daya jikinsu Yayi sanyi kaman an zare Musu Lakka ,kalamansa Sun saka Dukkansu Cikin Lissafi kowannensu ya shiga Tunani da iya lissafinsa Yana auna kalaman Ashman a lizamin Hankali.

Da Karfi ya ciji Lower lip dinsa Ya raɓa Saleem ta gefe zai fice, Ya jawo kwalar Rigarshi ta baya hakan ya tilasta Mishi Dakatawa  tare da juyowa yana kallonshi.

Sakin Rigansa yayi yace

“ idan Eye Glass ta fashe gangancin Mutum ne ya jawo hakan,Kuma matsiyaci ne zaiji  zafi saboda Tunanin yanda Zai sayi wani, Amma Idan Ta mai hali ce ba zai damu ba saboda mai Yiwuwa Yanada Spare ,Gilashin Ido badon Kara Kaifin gani kadai Ake sashi ba,ana sashi Domin kare Ganin Wasu Abubuwa Da kuma ado, Mai yiwuwa A yanda ka daukeni ka daukeni ne a matsayin madubi rufe ido hanani Ganin Hanya, Irin Wannan Gilashin Kuwa ko sun Fashe ba Damuwa mukeyi dasu ba don Dama ba Amfani Suke mana ba, Ashman..!, kai Likita ne Aikinka duba marasa Lafiya da yankasu, Wannan ake kira da Aikin Maza a ɓarka mutum a kuma ɗinke shi,Nima fa Karka manta Na iya Ɓarka Mutum,Na kuma san Matsalolin jinsin Da nake Ɓarkawa ta wani Ɓangaren Auren nan a sati Biyu Ba tilastama wani Akayi ba, Taimako zanyi ,Domin kuwa na riga na hango Abinda baka hango ba,  Zaifi dacewa da ka tsaya a inda Allah ya ajiyeka domin kuwa idan Kace zaka Shiga sabgar da ba naka ba Gilashin da yayi TsilliTsilli shi zaka taka batareda ka sani ba Ka kiyayi Shiga Sabgar Masoya babu Abinda bazai iya faruwa ba akan soyyaya....!"

Ya ƙarashe Maganar da Kallon cikin kwayar idon Ashman da yayi jazur kaman Wuta.

Ashman bai sake ce masa Komai ba ya fice a Fusace, Ashman yanada Fushi Sosai Amma abune mai wahala ganin fushinsa , idan yayi fushi kuma yakan dau lokaci kafin Ya sauko ,fushin sa nada Muni sosai Don Kuwa hatta wanda Bai kamata ya Shafa ba Saiya shafa.

Su Abba sun San halinshi Shiyasa Basu shiga tsakaninsu ba, yasan ya Zalunci Ashman dayawa,Ya kuma fifita saleem Akan Kowa ,Yasan Yanda Ashman Keji a Halin Yanzu Shiga tsakanin Fadarsu Bazai Sulhunta su ba sai dai abin ya dawo kanshi.

(_Da Ace ya kasance Uba na Gari Da Babu Abinda zaisa yaji Shakkar bawa 'dansa Umurni, Da Ya kwatanta adalci a tsakaninsu dukda kokwanto baishiga zuciyarsa ba, Ashman yana matuƙar ganin Girman Mahaifinsa zai iya bin duk hanyar da yace yabi matuƙar hakan bazai saɓama Shari'a ba, Amma Abba gani yake kaman Rashin Adalcin da yake nuna masa bazai sa yabishi ba, Yaji mamaki Matuƙa da ya Amince da Auren Deejah da kuma rage lokacin auren da akayi batareda ya tada Hayaniya ba ,Shiyasa a wannan Karon ya ƙyalesu Dukda ba tarbiyya bane fada a gaban Iyaye Especially a irin wannan taron da ya haɗa Gabaki daya ahali, Suma ganin Uban gayyan bai dakatar ba Shiyasa Suka zuba ido Suna kallo_ )

Shima Saleem Fita yayi Don Dama da Aiki a gabansa Kiran gaggawar da ya samu daga Abba ne yasashi Barin Komai ya taho.

Da fitan Salem sai daki ya Kacame da hayaniya jin Hayaniyan yayi yawa yasa Nurse dindake tareda Umma tayi Msu magana akan Su taimaka su Fita Don Mara lafiya na bukatar hutu.

Tunda saleem ya baro ward din  maganganun Ashman ne kawai ke yawo a zuciyar shi, gaba ɗaya ya kasa fahimtar alaƙar batun Aurensa da Misalin da ya kawo, Tabbas Ashman Nada Wani ɓoyayyen sirri a zuciyarshi, Kuma wannan Sirrin Zai iya daƙusar da Aurensu Cikin Ƙankanin Lokaci ,ya zama Dole ya gano Sirrin Gudun kar yayi Masa harin Sama.

Tunowa yayi da Irin Zurfin Cikin Ashman mai ban haushi , Koda faɗin Abu nada Muhimmanci a wajensa bai Cika fada ba , Ballantana ajega sirri.

  guntun tsaki yayi ya dan shafo gemunsa a hankali, yayinda idanunsa ke kallon hanya.

  Daga nan office ya koma, inda ya tarar da Maryam, Shigowar ta kenan ,gaba daya yau bai ganta ba,Dama idan Anyi Ward round aka maidata Labour ward ba cika ganinta yakeyi ba ,Saboda Akwai ayyuka achan sosai Basua zama .

Ta bishi da kallo tana sake kullum fuskar sa a haɗe babu rahama Shiyasa bata banbance yanayin Farin Cikinsa da kuma akasin hakan, tasan sarai ya ganta a tsaye Kuma shi ta take jira, amma ya basar abinsa..

Guntun Tsaki taja tana gyara Tsayuwarta, File din hannunta Ta dire Masa kan Desk Ta Kauda Kai Ko kallonshi batayi ba ta fara magana a Natse.

"Sir Wannan Patient ɗin ta samu cervical tear munyi Mata Episiotomy, Batada Kuɗin Biya so Sun Roƙi alfarmar a kara Musu time Mijin Matan Zaije ya nemo Zuwa Anjima, Na gayawa Incharge tace in Gaya maka in ka yarda , sannnan wannan Ta haihu lafiya but she's in Postpartum Haemorrhage She's in Severe Bleeding,Munyi iyakacin Ƙoƙarin mu don Ganin tayi Stopping Bleeding But Abin sai gaba Yakeyi So ur attention is neede...!"

"Really..!, Why Me?, Ina sauran Gynecologist na asibitin?, Ni kadai ne?"

"Sir You're The one Oncall Shiyasa na kiraka”

Tsawa ya daka mata
"Hang It,macen da ta samu Bleeding After birth Kema kinsan Mutuwa Zatayi inba anshawo kan Abinda Wuri ba, Na tabbata Zuwa iyanzu Jinin Ya rigada Ya taɓa internal Organs Dinta!, so kike in Ɓata Lokacina Akan Dying patient”

Yayi maganar Yana kallon Cikin idonta da ta fiddo waje a mugun mammakince.

"So Allow her to die in Peace ,Kinga Mun rage Msu nauyin Biyan treatment Fee, And Your first Complain Akan Me cervical Tear Dinnan Ku je ku kwance ɗinkin da Kukayi mata, Mijin yasan bazata iya haihuwa dakanta ba sai an ƙarata yaje ya Auro gajeruwar Mace, Duk wanda Ya Auro gajeruwar Mace yasani sarai wajen haihuwa Dole sai an Ƙarata saboda Nature of their Pelvis , So Ku kwance Ɗinkin Da Kukayi Kuma ku biya mu Stiches dinmu wannan Asibiti ne ba kasuwa ba da Za'ayi Aiki a bashi"

Ido Maryam ta Runtse da ƙarfi ta sa hannayenta Biyu ta toshe Kunnenta after Listening to the worse words of her life, Saleem Bashida Imani ko kadan ,Ta saba da ganin rashin Imaninsa Akan Mata marasa Lafiya Amma Nayau ya gigita ta Sosai, Inba Rashin Imani irin na saleem ba mace ta samu Yagewa wajen Haihuwa sun dinketa Mijinta Bashida Kudin Biya sai yace su kwance Ɗinkin kuma su biyasu kudin Stiches ɗin da sukayi amfani dashi?, wannan Wane irin Rashin Adalci ne?, Karshen Rashin Imani Shine wanda ya nuna akan dayan case din, Matar da ta haihu bayan ta haihu jini ya Ɓalle mata,Da gaskiyarsa idan aka kasa Dakatar da Zuban Jinin ana iya rasata ,Amma ai akan Shawo kan Matsalar In Allah Ya Nufa da sauran Rayuwa nan Gaba.

A karo na farko da ta fara taka masa Burki akan Rashin Imanin da yake nunawa Akan mata, Cikin Fusata tace

"Ba Iyawarka ta kawowa wannan Matsayin ba,A wannan Asibin aikinka shine kula da marasa Lafiya da Cetan Rayukansu, Babu ran kowa a hannunka baka isa ka tsaida Lokacin Mutuwan Wani ba Baka isa Hana mutuwa daukan Rai In Lokaci yayi ba, da har kake cewa baka son Kayi wasting Time ɗinka akan Dying patient Ka tuna Saida Uwarka tayi labor sannan Ta haifeka, Da Ace ta samu Postpartum Haemorrhage Anƙi dubata da Baka san Uwarka ba, don haka ko kaƙi ko kaso sai ka dubata ka kuma ceto Rayuwarta ,ka sani idan aka samu akasi ta rasa Rayuwarta saina Shigar da ƙara akanka, kuma kasan mafi kankancin Hukuncin da za'a zartar akanka shine su amshe License ɗinka ka dawo Normal Mutum kaman Kowa, Aikin da Kake taƙama dashi zai zamana babu Shi, and wacce mukayi ma Episiotomy kace mu Kwance bazamu Kwance ba, idan har ka isa Mara kunya kaje ka kwance dakanka sannan Ka cire Kudin Stiches daga cikin Albashin Mu,In ka cika mara Kunya Ka nuna Min Kai Isasshe ne!"

Ta ida atsawace hawaye na taruwa a idonta sosai kamar Wanda akace Uwarta ce akwance babu Lafiya.

Daukar files din tayi sannan ta watsa mishi wani kallon tsana da rinnanun idanunshi tabuga tsaki tareda fadin *"Bloody Bastard"*

Fuu tafice tana banging kofar da karfin tsiya.

Har tsakar kanshi yaji tsakinta ,kalamanta da kuma bugawar kofar.

Bin bayanta yayi da kallo kamar an ce masa zata dawo, zuciyarshi inbanda bugawa babu abinda take.

*_ko kaƙi ko kaso sai ka dubata ka kuma ceto Rayuwarta ,ka sani idan aka samu akasi ta rasa Rayuwarta saina Shigar da ƙara akanka, kuma kasan mafi kankancin Hukuncin da za'a zartar akanka shine su amshe License ɗinka ka dawo Normal Mutum kaman Kowa, Aikin da Kake taƙama dashi zai zamana babu Shi_*

Kalaman sai yawo suke masa, Tabbas Da gaskiyarta idan har ya saki Patient ɗin ta rasa Rayuwarta batareda ya dubata ba zai iya rasa license din Aikinsa Kuma hakan Ba karamin abin Kunya bane a gareshi.

Tsaki yaja ,Ya fita izuwa labor Ward din, Karo na farko a rayuwarsa Da yace bazaiyi abu ba kuma ya dawo yayi.

Yasha Matuƙar wahala kafin Ya ceto Rayuwarta don Saida Ya nemi taimakon colleagues dinsa, Koda Dayan Matan Kuma Yasa akayi mata uzuri har Mijinta ya samu Ya Kawo.

A ranar Wuni yayi Cikin ƙunci, Ashman Kuma yana Fita Bai Zarce ko'ina ba sai Company dinda yake Rulling Ya damƙa ragamar Komai a hannun Minal P.A dinsa, Sannan Ya dawo asibiti ya damƙa Komai a hannun Dr Aaryaan, Batare da ya gayawa kowa ba ya Kama Hanyar Lagos don a binciken da ya gudanar akan Bamalli an Gaya masa yana Lagos ,Abinda Ashman Bai sani ba kuwa Bamalli baya zama waje guda Duk inda yayi sai yayi barna daga nan Sai Ya Chanza Jaha, Bayayin Kwana biyu a gari idan ya jima ne yayi Kwana ɗaya.

Da yamma Su Kawu da tawagar su suka Koma Kano, Suka bar Abba Goggo, inna Yasai, Aeesh da Habeeb a Wajen Umma.

Yau Student doctors Dindake karkashin sa sunci ubansu don Sauke bala'in sa  saleem yayi akan su, Abu kadan saiya rufe mutum da fada kamar zai Dakesu, Sun saba da rashin Mutuncinshi Amma na yau ya fi Tsoratasu,Abin har Patient Relatives Saida Ya Shafa.

Ko maryam tsoron tunkarar Office din tayi don A yanda ta gasa Masa Magana Tasan Ya kamata kashinta ya bushe.

Haka yau Shift din yazama ran boss a6ace yake hakan yasa kowa ke taka tsantsan dashi, ta ɗayan Ɓangaren Kuwa Babu wanda yasan Big Boss ya bar Gari....

Bai sake Komawa duba Umma ba , Haka bai san Su Kawu sun Wuce ba.

Har dare yana Cikin Fushi, Jira yake Ashman ya dawo Ko ina yaje ya sauke masa Nasa Buhun Masifar yana ji idan bai Zazzage raguwan bala'in Akan Ashman ba ,Bazai taɓa samun natsuwa ba.

Shiru babu Ashman har Sha'dayan Dare, kasa haƙura yayi , har yayi Shirin Kwanciya bayan yayi wanka ya saka kayan barci, tashi yayi ya Cire su ya saka farar armless da Ƙaramar Nicker Iya Gwiwa.

Fridge ya buɗe ya dauko Kwalbar Wine dinsa ya kafa a baki, bai dire ba saida ya tabbata ya shanye, ya sake dauko wata kwalbar Nan ba saida ya shanye sannan ya dire, Kwalabe Uku Ya shanye a nan take, Cikin Kankanin Lokaci Yajishi a Chan sama , Ya fara Layi yana tangyaɗi

Jakar Goyo sa ya dauko Cikin Drawer ya kwashe Kwalaben barasan dake Fridge ya zubesu ciki, Sannan ya goya a bayanshi, Ya zura takalma ya fito yana Layi.

Karan Takunsa security Sukaji hakan yasasu Farkawa Suna rarumar Makami, Ganinshi yasasu maida makamai Suka buɗe masa Kofa Daya daga Cikin su ya biyo Bayansa, dama ya saba Duk Randa ya uni Cikin baƙin Ciki Sai ya fita daddare Yasha ya bugu sannan ya dawo don Kansa Idan ya bugu dayawa Kuma ya kwana a waje sai gari ya waje ya dawo yayita msu faɗa akan Meyasa Suka barshi Ya fita shi kadai?, Shiyasa Idan Abin nashi ya tashi suke bin Bayanshi gudun Abinda Zaije ya dawo,Dukda ba ƙaramin Wahala yake basu ba amma alherin Da sukesamu a wajenshi yafi ƙarfin wulakancin da yake mishi,ko kadan Ashman Baisan Saleem na shan komai ba don yana kiyayewa sosai wajen Ganin Ya kaucewa Ganinsa A duk lokacin da zaisha, Haka ya gargaɗi masu gadi akan Kada su sake Ashman ya samu labarin.

Garin Yayi Tsit bakajin Sautin Komai sai na tsuntsaye dake shawagi a sararin Samaniya.

Tafiya Sukeyi yana Kurɓar Barasarsa yana Zuba Surutai security dinda ke taka masa baya na Biye mashi.

Sunyi Tafiya mai nisa Sannan Suka Iso wani Eatery sai a lokacin Suka Fara ganin Mutane Kaman Ba dare ba, sai Shawagi suke suna kaiwa da dawowa Mafi yawancinsu mashaya ne da masu Fita taɗin dare da kuma matafiya da suka tsaya don Kore Yunwa Jakar bayansa Ya Sauke ya Fiddo Kwalabe ya diresu a gabansa.

_idan Kana tareda Ni nakan Hango Abinda Kai kanka baka hangowa a tattare da kai, Karka Sake Nayi tseren da zaisa Gilashin Ta kuɓuce ta faɗi ta Tarwatse ,Domin Kuwa koda ya Tarwatse na yarda Gangar Jikin Bazan bari Ragowar Fasassun Suyi Illa Ga Na kusa dani ba,Bansan Ya zaka dau zance na ba, Amma Ina umurtan Ka da ka gaggauta Kwance Kullalliyar da ka Kulla a zancen Aurenka da Ihkty...!_

Maganar Ashman yashiga amsa kuwa a kunnensa.

_Don haka ko kaƙi ko kaso sai ka dubata ka kuma ceto Rayuwarta ,ka sani idan aka samu akasi ta rasa Rayuwarta saina Shigar da ƙara akanka, kuma kasan mafi kankancin Hukuncin da za'a zartar akanka shine su amshe License ɗinka ka dawo Normal Mutum kaman Kowa, Aikin da Kake taƙama dashi zai zamana babu Shi, and wacce mukayi ma Episiotomy kace mu Kwance bazamu Kwance ba, idan har ka isa Mara kunya kaje ka kwance dakanka sannan Ka cire Kudin Stiches daga cikin Albashin Mu,In ka cika mara Kunya Ka nuna Min Kai Isasshe ne!_

Kalaman Maryam Suma suka gifta kwanyarsa

*_Bloody Bastard_*

Ya sakejin Zagin da tayi Mshi har Cikin Jikinsa Yaushe yayi lalacewar da mace zata Dubi tsabar idonshi Ta gasa masa Magana kuma ta tafi bai hukunta ta ba?,Ita wacece ?, waye Ubanta a wannan Garin?Meyasa Ta shigo Rayuwarsa?.

Wulla idanuwa yakeyi Yana kai Kwalba baki yana saukewa, bibbiyu Yake Gani Ammà hakan bai hana idanunshi Hango Mata wata Delivery Agent dinda ta sauka akan Machine ba, kanta sanye cikin Helmet Cikin nutsuwa ta bu'de Akwatin dake jogane da bayan Machine dinta ta Fiddo Wasu Ledoji sannan Ta shige ciki, Babu jimawa ta dawo ta sake kaiwa ciki ta fito tana Kirga kudi still hemlet din Rufe a kanta, Binta da kallo yayi tayi har ta Hau Machine ɗin Ta burga ta tafi.

Zumbur ya miƙe yabi bayanta, Mutumin da ya buyoshi na biye dashi ,Tayi masa nisa baya Hangota, Amma sai bin hanyar yake dake miƙakkiyar hanya ce.

Sunyi nisa sosai babu Ita babu Alamarta, Hakan Yasashi Juyawa yana fadin

Baffa dake ƙoƙarin zubama kansa Malt a kofi ya sake katseShi da faɗin

 “ina Zaka ba'a tashi Taro ba?”.

  Ya kai ƙarshen maganar yana Kurɓar Malt din 

Juyowa yy a hankali,idanunsa jajur ya ɗago ta kalle Abba, shima kallon nashi yake, amma sai Abba ya janye nasa idanun ganin yana neman rikitashi, 

 Murmushin yaƙe Yayi a Cikin bazata yace

“Ga Ihty Kuyi yanda Kuke so da Ita , Ba gidan Ka ba, Ko a dakin ka ne Kasata, Abba Kayi Yanda Kaso, Amma Ka sani Na harhaɗa komai Kiris ya Rage ...!Ka gaya Min A baya Hujja Ake buƙata domin a kama Me Laifi Hakan yasa Na zama Mai ɓoye sirrin Kowanne a cikinku !, Amma ba shiru nayi Ba Motsi nakeyi cikin Duhu"


Cikin ɗan ruɗewa da kalaman Ashman na bazata Abba ya maida Dubansa Ga yan dakin da a halin yanzu Shi Suke kallo, Suna binshi da kallon tuhuma.


Gira ɗaya Ashman ya ɗage sama, ya janye idanunsa da ya fara canja launi ba tare da ya tankama Sake Furta komai ba, cikin nuna halin ko in kula ya mai da hankalinsa ga Baffa

“Tabbas bai kamata in Tafi ba, Ya kamata In tunasar daku Abinda Kuka manta,  Naso na Tafi na Bar su Daku ta yanda zaku Fahimta ,Amma da Alama so Kuke Kowa Yasan komai"

Nan Take Hankulan Kowa Ya sake Karkata akansa Taɓe Baki yayi Shifa ya tsani Kallo, Shiyasa Ya tsani taro Ko wanne ne,fuska a ɗaure yana wani hura hanci da fesar da iska yace

"Abba Mema kace?, cancantar Da mutum ke bukata domin FarinCikin Aure shine haƙuri?"

Yyi Maganar Yana Takowa Gaban Abba Da ya Koma Tamkar Gunki, banda Zufa babu Abinda Ke karyo masa


"Bayan Wannan Akwai Wani Abu BABA ,Kun Gani a tarayyar mu *Shaukin Tarayya* shine Cancanta Mafi Dacewa , Ya kamata Ka gane cewa Shaukin Tarayya Yafi Soyayya girma, Rayuwar Aure Mai Cikeda Shauki Take Buƙata ,Ka Aura Mata Shalelenka don Yana Sonta Da Ace Akwai Shaukin Tarayya da bai Saketa akan Abinda bai Kai ya kawo ba"

Dubansa Yakai kan Saleem Dake Duƙe a kasa Ya kasa Ɗagowa Ya haɗa Ido dashi

"Ba Tilastawa ko Auren  Wacce Kake so bane ,Shauki na nufin Nuna soyayya a aikace !, kowa Cewa Yake yana Soyayya Amma nawa ne ke nuna Soyayyar Su a aikace?  , Soyayya bashine Samun Wacce Kake So ba, Kaso Asoka sannan kuma ka kaddamar mata da su ne cikin farin ciki ta hanyar da dakanta zata Gamsu dakai ta kuma Amince"

Sake kafe saleem da Idanunsa Yayi ya kankantar da Murya

"Inda Ace ka kwantar dakai ka Nuna Mata Soyayya a aikace, da Ka ɗanɗana Zallar Soyayya, Nasan Ihty Batada Matsala Akwaita da Tausayi Zata Soka da Aminci, Idan ka kasa Iyawa da Kanka Babu wanda Zai iya maka, ma'anar Aure tsakanin Mutane biyu ma'ana ce ta Farinciki , Amma kuma sai taka ta kare a Gantale ma'ana Baka san Darajar Auren ba ma"

Dan Dakatawa yayi Yana jin kansa na cigaba da sara masa Sosai kaman Zata Rabe gida Biyu, Cikin dakakkiyar Muryarsa Ya cigaba da faɗin

"Madadin a koya Maka yanda Zaka gane darajar Aure?, Nan da kwanaki kana zuwa Masu da zancen Maidata Zakaga an goya maka baya ,An fara mata hudubar tayi hakuri,ita ba Mutum bace?, Meyasa Bazakuyi considering Nata Feelings dinba?, Nasan Taron nan Tun farko ba don haka aka Hadashi ba, Taramu Akayi domin a maida ma Saleem matarsa, ba don Matar Uncle Imam tasa baki ba sa yanzu ana shirin kai ta"

Idanunsa Ya rintse da Karfi

"Gani nake kaman tilasta ta Kuke tayi Rayuwar da babu Ruwan Wani !"

Duban sa Ya maida Kan Abba

"Banaso Ihty tayi irin Rayuwan nan Tareda Abokin Rayuwar ta,So nake su shaƙu, Su Samu matukar Yanci da shaukantuwa da juna"

Tsawaitawa Yyi yana Maida Numfashi kaman zaki, Ashman bai cika Dogon magana ba, yafi ganewa Ya dunkule magana Duk Girman Ta , Abinda zaisa Yayishi dalla dalla ba Karamin Abu bane.

Tsitt ɗakin yayi bayan tsawatarwar da akayi,Cigaba yayi da 

"Ihty mace ce da ta iya Rayuwa, domin Tabbatar da farinCikin wani takan iya sadaukar da nata ,Ihty Mutum ce wacce tasan da cewa Shauki yanada matuƙar Muhimmanci , Tareda Sanin cewa Soyayya nada Matuƙar Illa Musamman idan kana so ba'a Sonka Shiyasa Ta sadaukar da nata jin dadin Ta yarje wa Auren wanchan shashashan, Amma da yake Shi Gaula ne Baisan Ni'imar da Allah yayi masa ba sai ya Banzatar da Alfarmarsa, ka kasance tare da Mutum ya nuna mata Soyayya suyi hira dashi mara ƙarewa Course ne na Shauƙi mara Ƙarewa"

Takowa yayi Gaban Baffa, Hakan Yasashi Saurin Miƙewa ,ya dire Cup din Malt ɗinsa A Hankali

"Me ma kace kawu?, Taro bai Kare ba?, tabbas Taro bai Kare ba, Kuma idan Ina wuri Bazai taɓa ƙarewar ba ,Akan Mutuwar Auren da Babu Soyayya kuma da Kokarin tauye hakkin Wacce bataji ba bata gani ba zan iya kwana Ina Jawabi, Rashin sanin Wadannan Abubuwan Shiyasa na boye sirrina domin Na bashi dama, Yanzu Cikin Alfahari a gaban Ahali gaba ɗaya na fada zan kuma maimaita *Bana Son A'isah* !, Ayi Mata aure da wanda Take so Yake Sonta wanda , Wanda Zasu Kasance tare da juna Cikin Shauki Tareda Hira mara Karewa!, zuciya takan Ba mutane da dama Dama, Amma Dama Ta haƙika Mutum daya Ake bawa Ita...!"

Komawa Yayi ya zauna A Kujerar da ya tashi, Ya ɗora kafa ɗaya bisa Ɗaya

"Mutane da Dama Suna Sonta,, Amma Ita tanada wanda Yake Sonta da gaskiya kuma Tana Sonshi,, Na fahimci akwai Shauƙi na tarayya a tattare dasu , Na yarda Ihty ta zauna anan har Yan gidan su Sudawo ,Kafin wannan Lokacin Ni zan Tabbatar Muku da Wanda Yafi dacewa da ita ... Har yanzu labarin bai fara Ba!, Wannan Somantaɓi ne"


Idanunsa ya rumtse da ƙarfi saboda Sarawan Da kansa yayi masa A lokaci guda.

Sam babu wanda jikinsa baiyi sanyi ba, Gashi Duk iya auna Kalamansu Basu Gano Asalin Inda Zantukansa Suka nufa ba.

Tafi ASHRAFF ya farayi Cikin karfafa gwiwa yace

"Tabbas Saleem Bai cancanci Rayuwa da Aeesh ba, Shiyasa tun bai Shiga daki Ba Allah ya kawo karshen zamansu, a barta tayi Rayuwa Kaman Kowa!,a barta taji da Abinda ke Damunta ,and Nima na Bana Goyon bayan Ta cigaba Da Zama da Me mata dayan Nan Domin kuwa Takura msu Zatayi ,nan Dinne yafi dace wa da Ita”

 "Kai Yaya Anya Maan zai iya Rayuwa babu ita kuwa?, Ni sai gani nake kaman ciccika baki ne kawai Irin nashi”

Cewar Abdul yana kallon Ashman A kaikaice

"Ni Bama Wannan ba, Ni So nake a maida ma Yaya Saleem Matarsa Asirin kowa a rufe, tunda Yana Sonta"

Inji Nafeesah

"Eh mana ban Taba Ganin Kukan Yaya Saleem ba sai a kanta, ji yanda Nagartaccen Namiji Irin Yaya Yake Durƙushe a ƙasa With his Pride lowered Duka Akan Mace, mutumin Da mata basu Gabanshi ,gaskiya idan Anbi ta Yaya Suff an cuci Saleem"

Cewar Zee Cikin Rashin Kunya.

 Tsawa Ashraff ya daka mata wadda ta sakasu firgita har tana neman rasa Nutsuwanta

"Shi Tsararki ne?"


Tsaki mai ƙarfi Ashman yaja yana Miƙewa, dama zuciyarsa a saman maƙoshi take sosai.

Duban Deejah Yayi

"Mu je"

Miƙewa Tayi tabi bayansa ranta fesss,Taku yake a hankali har ya fita

Binshi Da kallo Aeesh tayi ta kasa Motsawa Daga inda take,Hawaye na Gangarowa saman Kumatunta,ta kasa dakatar dashi Har ya Fice, Bata Son ya Tafi ya barta, bata son Zama dasu Ko kadan, bata Ƙaunar Kaddarar Da zaisa ta sake zama Cikin wannan Ahalin a karo na biyu.

Kamar ruwa ya cinyesu haka falon yay tsitt suka bishi da kallo har ya fice.

 tsitt falon yayi banda Kukan Aeesh babu Abinda ke tashi a dakin, Babu Abinda ya sata Kuka face Yanda Ashman ya fada a gaban Kowa cewa Baya Sonta , Kuma Ya yafeta ga Su Abba.
      
A masifance Umma Saude ta taso ma Ashraff Da masifan akan Me Zai daga ma 'ya'yanta Murya don Sun fadi Gaskiya...!, shima Tsawan ya daka Mata Cikin nuna mata Tsantsar Gaskiyan dake tattare da kalaman Ashman batareda Yayi la'akari da Ita din Mahaifiyar sa bace.

 take falon ya hautsine da hayaniya, Aeesh dai tana daga gefe ta Takure waje Guda tana kallonsu, sai dai bata fahimtar komai.

Abu kamar wasa saiga gidan ya harmutse sosai, Matar Kawu Imam sai fama take dasu amma sunƙi suyi shiru, ganin hakanne ya sakata Ficewa ta ƙyalesu, Aeesh kuma ta fashe musu da kuka mai karfi.


      
Tsitt falon yayi , Cikeda Tausayin ta Qawwamah tayo kanta Ashraff kuma ya fice abinsa rai ɓace, da sauri Naseer yabi bayansa, Abba da Baffa ma suka fice, a sanyaye Saleem yabi bayansu.

A fusace Zee da Nafeesah suka hauta da masifa,batareda Sunyi la'akari da halin Tashin Hankalin da take ciki ba kiyayya ce sosai na hango a idanun Kowanne a cikinsu, In ka cire Qawwamah Ƙanwar ASHMAN
     
Wannan lamari nasu yayi matuƙar bani mamaki sosai, har yamma suna Rikici, Abinda bai taka Kara ya karya ba sai Su hau Fada akai, Dama Haka Ahalin Yake Kullum Cikin hayaniya, idan Kajisu shuru to babu Nafeesah Da Zee a gidan.

Har dare Aeesh taƙi ci taƙi sha sai Kuka, tayi Ta Gwada kiran Ashman Amma baya dauka daga Karshe sai ya koma Rejecting, Da ta dameshi ma Dauka yayi ya hauta Masifa tare da cewa idan Ta sake kiranshi Sai Ranta Yayi mummunan Ɓaci sai Yayi mata Abinda Bazata taɓa mantawa dashi ba.

Tsoro ,mamaki da Fargaba Duk Suka shigeta tunda take Ashman bai taɓa yi mata Magana cikin Irin wannan Yanayin ba, a iya Kalaman sa ta Fahimci zai iya aikata Alwashin sa akanta idan bata Kiyayeshi ba, Zata iya cewa wannnan Ranar Shine Rana Mafi Muni a rayuwar ta ,bazata Taɓa manta wannan Ranar ba sai a yanzu ta gaskata Ammi Da tace Batada Kowa A duniyan nan Sai ita Da habeeb su kadai Suka rage mata a rayuwa,da Ace Wani ne ya gaya mata Ashman Zai iya cewa ya rabu da ita kenan Na har abada da nan take za Ta ƙaryata shi.

Shima Ashman A ranar Baccin Bakin Ciki yayi Cikeda tarin Tausayi da Tsanar AEESH, Domin Kuwa Abba ya taɓo masa Tabon da ya jima da sakayawa dukda bai Warke Ba ,Abba ya tuna masa Da mummunar Tabon Da yayi wa Aeesh a idanunsa Wanda bazai taɓa mantawa ba,Wannan Karon sosai yakejin ya tsaneta dama Duk wanda Yayi kokarin Tunkararsa a wannan Lokacin!.


Saleem Ma Tattara kayanshi yayi ya bar masa Gida Ya koma babban Gida domin Kuwa Haduwan su a wannan Ranar saida Ya kusa Saɓa masa kamanni, haka kawai daga dawowansa masallaci sukaci karo ya Kamashi da duka Duk inda ya samu kai hannu yake ,Cikin kankanin Lokaci ya haɗa masa Targaɗe Ta ko ina da kyar Securities suka kwaceshi a hannunsa.

Koda ya koma ciki kulle kansa yayi a ciki a lokacin kiran Aeesh yayita Shigowa, cikin wannan Yanayin ya Sauke mata Nata buhun Masifar Tareda kwakkwarar Kashedi akan ta fita daga Rayuwarsa Kamar yanda Shima ya fita a nata Rayuwar kenan Har Abada✍️


Yana nan a dakin Har dare Bai buɗe Kofa Ba,Duk Bugun da Deejah tayi tayi Bai buɗe ba, hakan yasa Hankalinta ya tashi matuƙa ta Fita ta aika mai gadi Ya kira masu Ɓalla kofa, nan Take aka kirasu aka ɓalla Kofar.


Ga mamakinsu ya jiƙe sharkaf da zufa dukda sanyin AC dake aiki a ɗakin, gashi idanunsa sun kaɗa sunyi jazur har ana iya hango yanda jijiyoyin kansa sukai ruɗu-ruɗu, Ko ina Na dakin Yayi kaca kaca Da Alama shine ya Tarwatsa su.

Cikeda tsoro ta zo gareshi ta na Rirriƙeshi da faɗin

"Baby me ke damun ka? ,zaka Illata kank....."

Bata ida Fadar abinda ke bakinta ba taji saukar Wani Gigitaccen Mari Wanda yasata ja baya babu Shiri,Idanunta saida suka juya saboda gigicewa

Kafin ta dawo Hayyacinta ya nufota da faɗin

"Me tayi maka ka Kashe ta?, Me Umma na Tayi maka Kasa Aka kashe ta?”

Jada Baya ta Farayi a matuƙar Tsorace,Binta yakeyi Yana Maimaita "Meyasa Ka Kashe ta?"


Sosai Zuciya ya fara rawa,ya jiƙe sharkaf da zufa, gashi idanunsa sun kaɗa sunyi jazur kamar Wuta har ana iya hango yanda jijiyoyin kansa sukai ruɗu-ruɗu, hannayensa Da Jijiyoyi ke a mimmike ya Dunƙule.

      
"baby mike damunka?”
 Deejah ta faɗa da tsagwaron Tashin Hankali cikin karfin hali, tana Rufe baki taji saukan naushi a Kumatunta wanda Yayi sanadiyyar Zubowar biyu daga Cikin Haƙoranta na gefe.


"Saina Aikaka inda Ka aikasu, kasheka zanyi, Sai ka bisu....”

 wani mugun tsalle Deejah ta yi ,ta kauce masa, amma dukda haka saida ya Sake kai mata wani Naushin a fuska, ihu ta fasa ta fara neman hanyar Fita don Tsira da Rayuwarta.



Deejah Bata san hawaba bata san saukaba kawai ya hauta da duka, tun tana ƙoƙarin kare kanta hatna gaza dan Ba karamin Ƙarfi ya Tara mata ba, gashi kuma bayan Security dinda Ya Buɗe Kofar babu mai karɓarta, shima anan Ya samu nasa Rabon Aka Targaɗe Masa hannu.

        
Tun Deejah na fahimtar abinda ke faruwa harta sume, amma Ashman Baya Cikin Hayyacinsa idanunsa ya rufe bare ya fahimci bata numfashi.


Wani irin Gurnani yake yana iƙirarin kashe Saleem,A cewarsa Deejah ce SALEEM har Shima ya yanke jiki ya faɗi ƙasa babu numfashi


Nan fa hankalin security dinda ya ɓalla Kofar ya tashi, ya dau Keypad din Ashman Da y Gani yashe a ƙasa ,yayi dialing Numbern Abba ,Bugu ɗaya ya daga a tsorace yake sanar dashi Halin da Ake ciki, babu Bata lokaci Abba Ya aika a Kira Psychiatrists Dama Yasan Za'a Rina Tunda Yaga yana Zubo zance a meeting, Ya Kuma yi ma Saleem Duka yasan Abin Ya Motsa.

Tun Rasuwar Mahaifiyar Abin Yaso ya taɓashi Idan makamancin abinda ya faru a Lokacin Rasuwar Mahaifiyar tasa Ta kasance, Sai ya Firgice ya fara abu Kaman Mahaukaci, idan kuma akayi Sa'a da Mutum a kusa dashi sai ya kusa Hallakashi Sannan zai Kyaleshi, Babu mai iya shawo kanshi sai ya suma ..

Da gangar Abba ya tara Ahali ya Kuma kawo maganar Da tayi daidai Da komawan Aeesh wajenshi,Sai Kuma akayi Sa'a Saleem ya dawo a daidai lokacin hadari ke taruwa wanda hakan Ya sake Birkita Lissafi..

Sosai Likitoci suka Hau bashi taimakon gaggawa, kwanakin sa Biyu a kwance ko motsawa bayayi, a rana ta Uku Ya farka kaman Bashi Ba.


Daga wannan Ranar Ya fara Kiyaye duk wata Kaddarar Da zata Hadasa da Gidan Su Abba domin Kuwa hakan Shine mafi alheri a gareshi, Lissafin Abba yazo masa Daidai domin Kuwa haka ya tsara.

A Cikin kwanaki Biyun da yayi a kwance Aeesh tasha Satan hanya da Niyyar Zuwa dubashi Ana kamata,Sun hanata Fita ko ina Sun hanata mu'amala da kowa, So biyu Reey na zuwa Gidan Ana hanata Ganinta A cewar su tana Idda Kuma haramun ne Zantawa da Kowa indan Ana Idda...✍🏼



Tun bayan Wayan da Sukayi da Ammi a ranar da zasu taho Gidan Su Abba ,bata sakejin Komai daga Garesu ba, Dama babu wanda ya damu Da halin Da Ammie ke ciki sai Ashman, Shi kadai ya damu da ya kira yaji Lafiya Gashi Yanzu an Rabata dashi .

Rayuwarta A gidan Su Abba Kaman Rayuwa Cikin Wuta , bata Da Yancin Taɓa Komai na gidan saida Izinin Umma Saude Sun Maidata Bazawarar Dole kullum Kulle take a daki Batada yancin Fitowa waje tasha Iska duk a cewar su Idda Takeyi.


Mazan Gidan Har mantawa Sukayi da Akwai Wata A'isha dake zaune a gidan Inba labari akeyi aka sakota a ciki ba.


Saleem Kuwa saida yayi Jinyar sati Biyu sannnan Ya fara fita, a A ranar da ya fara Fita kuwa Duk inda Yabi sai an Nunashi ance ga Angon Da ya saki Matarsa a daren Farko Tun kan Ya shiga daki.


Bayan Biki da sati Uku Ya koma Asibiti, har a lokacin Ashman bai koma ba, yana dai Zuwa Office dinsa, Kuma ya hana Deejah Aiki a asibitin Sa Yace zai dinga biyanta Albashi Kwatankwacin yanda Yake biyanta a da ,ba haka Taso ba Amma dole ta Amince don yanzu ya fara Sakin Mata fuska dukda ba sosai ba, har abincinta yanaci, Badon komai ba sai don Tausayin Dukan da yayi mata a madadin Saleem ,Da ya dawo Hayyacinsa Aka sanar dashi✍🏼.



Rashin Zuwan Ashman wajen Aiki shi ya sake ba Saleem Daman gwada Rashin Mutuncinsa Akan ma'aikata Yanda Yaga Dama, Wulaƙanci Kuwa Sai abinda Ya ƙaru, baya ganin Kowa da gashi Da zarar kayi masa Laifi zaisa a sallameka in Kayi Sa'a Kuma ya Wulaƙanta ka gashi Yasa A Ninka ma Ma'aikata Albashi ta yanda Zaiyi Tsiyarsa Yanda Yaga Dama , Dukda Saida Akayi ta zama Kafin A yarda da karin Albashin Amma Abba shi ya tsaya Mai kan Komai.



*Maryam*

Yau Da Baƙin Ciki ta tashi Tsakanin Jiya zuwa yau Kajin Ta Sun kai ashirin Wa'enda Suka mutu ga marasa Lafiya Sunfi Goma.


Shiryawa takeyi a Sanyaye Fuskar ta Duk ta Kumbura Alamar tasha Kuka.

Sneakers dinta ta sa, Bayan tasaka uniform dinta, ta rataya jakarta sannan ta Fito cikin Sanyin jiki.


Mamace zaune tana Kasa Wainar safiya, Siddika na Zaune Gabanta sai Zuba surutu take

A wuce ta tayi tana cewa

"Mama sai na dawo"

"A'a Tsaya ki karya mana"

Kamar xata yi kuka tace

"Na koshi"

 Mama ta bude ido sosai tace 

"A'a toh ke kuma baki da tsoron Allah, kika koshi a Ina? Uban me kika ci?,Jiya ma Ina Kallonki Kika ba Auta Yar Awarar da Nayi dadaddare, oho Ni babu Ruwa na  kije can ke Likita ce kinfini sanin Illar ulcer a cikin jikin Mutum Inaji ana cewa Ulcer tsittsinka Hanji yakeyi, Ohow Ina ruwa Na Kika kashe kanki banida Asara Inada Siddika da Salima Me Zai dameni?, ga mijina Lafiyayye Zai iya samar min da wasu Bayan Siddi"

Turo baki Maryam Tayi ta cigaba da takawa, Saleema Ce ta shigo Tana Cewa

 "Mama Kaji Uku Cikin Manyan nan sun kara ware wa"


"Innalillahi waina ilaihi rajiun" Maryam tafada ahankali tana lumshe idanu ga mammakin kowa hawaye suka ga ya silalo daga idanunta.

A mammakince mama tace

"A'a mero Meye haka?, kuka kikeyi akan Kaji?"


Aikuwa kaman jira take saita fashe da kuka mai sauti, harda shesheka kamar wanda akayima tsinannan duka.

Sakin baki mama tayi tana kallonta.

Siddiqa Karamar Ƙanwarta ma ganin Antynsu na Kuka yasata Fashewa Kuka mai sauti.

Saleema Dake tsaye tana Kallon ikon Allah dariya ta Kwashe dashi harda tafawa don ita Bataga Abin Kuka ba Don kaji sun Mutu, a cewar ta Lokacin su ne yayi.


"Tabdi yau naga Ikon Allah, Akan Kajin banza kinzo zaki tada Ma Mutane hankali?,Kinga Idan Kuka Zakiyi ga bokiti chan Ki Tara In ya cika, ki tara wani ShashaSha''


Bayan Hannu tasa Ta Share Hawayen ta, Ta dubi Siddika Daketa Faman Rera kuka cikin Turo baki tace

"Auta...!, dauko Min bokiti 😭, Kila sai na cika mata shi zata gane zafin da Nakeji a Zuciyata"

Murfin Coolarn wainan dake gabanta Ta jefe ta  dashi

"Kinci gidan Ku dake Da Kajin!, Kika bani haushi kafin ki dawo zan sa Sama'ila Ya yanka Min Dukka ingani ya Zakiyi sakaryar banza"


Fita maryam tayi Tana Cewa

" Wlh aka taɓa min Kajina za'a ga tashin Hankali"





ALHAMDULILLAH
